金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 10 月 13 日
上週重點訊息
◼ TOPIX 啟動大規模改革：日本交易所集團 10 月 7 日公布 TOPIX 調整結果，符合新制納入標
準的成分股將由 9 月底的 1,636 檔降至 986 檔，另有 35 檔股票新納入。現有成分股中，共
有 683 檔未符合流動性及浮動市值等標準，這些股票將自 2026 年 10 月底起逐步降低權
重，並於 2027 年 10 月重新評估，屆時仍未達標者將於 2028 年 7 月底降至零。此次改革有
助於改善 TOPIX 成分股的流動性及投資價值，並促使企業更加重視市場評價。

◼ 巴西總統選舉波索納洛首輪領先魯拉：巴西最高選舉法院 10 月 5 日公布首輪投票結果，參
議員弗拉維奧・波索納洛取得 47.03%的有效票，現任總統魯拉獲得 45.16%，兩人均未取得
過半票數，將於 10 月 25 日進行第二輪決選。若最終由波索納洛當選將相較有利於巴西股市
表現。
◼ IEA 支持加速釋出既有儲油：國際能源署（IEA）10 月 7 日表示，會員國支持加快釋出 3 月
已承諾提供的儲油，並將優先增加柴油供應。截至目前，會員國已釋出約 3.25 億桶儲油；若
剩餘承諾全數執行，可再向市場提供約 1 億桶供應，有助緩解運輸業與農業面臨的燃料成本
壓力。隨各國持續釋出戰略儲備石油，將有利於在短期內壓抑全球油價進一步上行。
市場展望
◼ 標普 500 獲利預估連續上修，資訊科技為主要推升來源：標普 500 企業獲利預估連續兩季上
修，其中資訊科技產業為主要改善來源，反映 AI、雲端運算及資料中心需求仍然強勁。雖然
美國資料中心仍面臨電力與基礎設施瓶頸，但建置規模持續擴大，AI 投資也逐步延伸至記憶

體、網路設備、資安及企業應用等領域。因此，預期科技類股的獲利動能仍具支撐，具 AI 供
應鏈優勢的台股與日股亦可望延續相關成長動能。
◼ 中天期債券可望兼顧息收與利率風險：美國經濟仍具韌性，加上政府與企業發債供給維持高
檔，可能使市場利率維持在較高水準。雖然投資級債信用利差位於相對低檔，但企業獲利、
現金流及償債能力仍具支撐，較高的美國公債基準利率也使其利息收益維持一定水準。短天
期債券面臨利率下降後的再投資風險，長天期債券則較易受到公債供給及利率波動影響，因
此中天期美國公債及投資級債可望在息收與利率風險之間維持較為平衡的表現。

AI 資本支出牽動股市，債市關注 Fed 政策走向
2026 年 11 月
10 月全球主要股市普遍回落，主要受累於 AI 資本支出攀升與融資壓力引發科技股評價修正。以
美元計價的報酬來看，MSCI 新興亞洲下跌 8.6%最弱，新興市場跌 8.1%；其中韓國 KOSPI 單月重
挫 16.2%、台股跌 5.7%，反映市場重新評估 AI 投資回收效益，半導體股首當其衝。MSCI 拉丁美洲
則下跌 7.7%，因巴西總統魯拉連任後財政紀律疑慮升高 。另一方面，MSCI 印度上漲 1.2%表現最佳，
受企業獲利與資本利得稅調降預期支撐 。歐股則受惠於資金轉向醫療保健等防禦性類股 ， 加上能源股

受油價上漲帶動，抵銷了銀行類股的跌勢，惟歐元貶值也壓縮了其以美元計價的表現。
聯準會 10 月雖維持利率不變，但全球債市轉由避險需求主導，AI 投資融資及信用疑慮促使資金
流向公債，美國 10 年期公債殖利率由 9 月底 5.29%降至 10 月底的 4.94%。全球主權債上漲 1.34%
居冠 ， 新興美元債與全球投資級公司債分別上漲1.26%、1.18%。 惟信用利差擴大抵銷部分利率紅利，
聖路易 Fed 資料顯示，美國高收益債利差(ICE BofA US High Yield Index OAS)由 9 月底的 312 bps
升至 10/29 的 326bps，導致全球高收益債僅漲 0.50%，亞洲高收益債更下跌 0.95%。新興當地債
10 月下跌 0.64%，主要受到巴西選後財政疑慮及匯率波動拖累。
在股市方面，儘管10 月科技股因 AI 資本支出與投資回收疑慮出現明顯修正，但全球企業獲利表
現仍具韌性。隨著 AI 基礎建設持續擴張，HBM、伺服器及先進製程等相關供應鏈仍具中長期成長動

能 ； 然而 ， 市場關注焦點已由資本支出規模轉向投資效益與自由現金流 ， 企業獲利能否符合高度預期 ，
將成為影響股價表現的關鍵。整體而言，美國、台灣及日本等具備 AI 供應鏈與科技產業優勢的市場
仍值得關注，惟 11 月美國期中選舉結果、聯準會政策預期及能源價格變化，仍可能牽動市場風險偏
好，預期後續股市將呈現獲利基本面主導、產業與個股表現分化的格局。
就債市而言，10 月下旬避險買盤帶動美國 10 年期公債殖利率回落至約 4.94%，但聯準會政策方
向仍偏緊。10 月 FOMC 以 10 比 2 維持利率於 3.75%～4.00%，兩名反對者均主張升息 1 碼，市場
對 12 月升息的預期機率已逾七成，加上多位官員釋出鷹派訊號，短期殖利率仍有反彈風險。另一方
面，美國經濟成長具韌性，財政赤字及公債供給壓力亦可能限制長天期殖利率下行。投資級債方面，
雖具票息收益優勢，但 AI 企業融資與新債供給增加，仍可能使信用利差承壓。整體而言，中天期美
國公債及信用品質良好的投資級公司債，仍具中期收益吸引力，惟升息預期尚未消退，應審慎控管存

續期間風險，並留意通膨、聯準會政策及信用利差變化等因素。

美國股市：獲利成長支撐基本面，AI 投資回收與高利率牽動評價
10 月美股走勢受到大型科技股評價、通膨預期及企業財報交互影響，標普 500 指數在月內呈現
震盪 ，指數領漲產業亦輪動 。 月初AI 需求支撐大型科技股 ； 中旬隨能源價格上揚及美債殖利率波動，
部分高本益比股票面臨獲利了結。月底大型科技公司公布財報後，市場對高資本支出的疑慮升溫，科
技股賣壓擴大 。10 月 26 日至 30 日標普 500 下跌 0.57%， 其中通訊服務與資訊科技分別下跌5.32%
及 2.03%，醫療保健、原物料及公用事業則分別上漲 3.12%、2.79%及 2.04%。資金由科技權值股

轉向防禦性及傳統產業，指數內部表現分化。
美國總經方面，10 月 S&P Global 綜合 PMI 初值維持擴張，企業活動具韌性，但房市等利率敏
感產業承壓。能源價格走高使通膨回落存在不確定性。聯準會於10 月 28 日 FOMC 會議決議維持聯
邦資金利率於 3.75%至 4.00%，符合市場預期，並將經濟活動評估上修為強勁；兩名官員則支持升
息一碼，突顯決策層對物價壓力的看法仍有差異。主席於記者會指出，先前政策緊縮已逐步影響家庭
及企業的信用條件 ， 資本配置也趨向審慎 。 會後升息預期與美債殖利率波動 ， 牽動科技股評價 。 就業、
薪資及通膨走勢仍是政策關鍵。
2026 年第三季美股企業財報整體維持成長，資訊科技與能源為獲利預估上修的主要來源。10 月
初研究顯示，資訊科技產業第三季每股盈餘預估上修 3.5%，能源產業上修 18%，但原物料與部分消
費類股遭下調，產業間獲利趨勢並不一致。不過，市場對企業獲利的檢視逐步由營收及訂單成長，延
伸至設備折舊、資本支出、融資負擔及自由現金流。部分公司即使業績優於預期，仍因財測保守或現

金流偏弱而承壓 。 大型科技股價反應取決於資本支出與獲利轉換效率 。 相較過去單純以營收成長衡量
科技需求，市場開始檢視折舊負擔對淨利率的影響。
展望 11 月，美股企業獲利仍可提供中期支撐，但利率維持高檔、AI 相關評價偏高，以及政策不
確定性，可能使主要指數短期波動加劇。產業配置維持大型科技核心部位，優先考量具技術壁壘、穩
定客戶需求及自由現金流的企業 ； 對資本支出增幅明顯高於營收成長 、 融資需求較高或估值已充分反
映成長預期的個股 ， 則宜採取較保守的部位管理 。 半導體與雲端供應鏈仍須區分訂單能見度與投資回
收期。醫療保健及公用事業需求較穩，可能吸引防禦性資金。後續變數包括科技企業財測、通膨、就
業與期中選舉後政策方向。

歐洲股市：法國財政與能源風險升溫，留意殖利率對經濟與股市之影響
10 月泛歐指數震盪收高，較9 月底 634.89 點上漲約 1.87%，為 6 月以來最佳單月表現。月初受
到油價攀升、歐洲公債殖利率走高及法國財政疑慮干擾，指數承壓，銀行與利率敏感類股表現偏弱；
隨後企業財報陸續公布，能源與國防相關企業獲利支撐市場反彈，醫療保健等防禦性產業亦受到青
睞，部分藥廠上調營運展望後股價走強。下旬指數一度升抵逾一個月高點，惟月底美國科技企業財報
引發 AI 投資回收疑慮 ， 歐洲半導體設備與科技股跟隨下挫 。10 月漲勢呈現產業輪動與個股財報分化 。

歐元區經濟維持擴張但動能趨緩 。 第三季GDP 初值季增 0.4%， 低於第二季的0.6%， 年增1.3%；
10 月綜合 PMI 初值由 9 月 53.1 降至 51.9，反映企業新訂單成長放慢，法國罷工、政治動盪及融資
成本上升亦壓抑商業活動。能源價格推升投入成本，企業轉嫁能力卻未同步增強，形成成長放緩與物
價壓力並存的局面，企業招聘步調也趨於保守。ECB 於 10 月 29 日維持政策利率不變，指出能源衝
擊可能同時提高通膨並抑制成長，且融資環境已較前次會議緊縮。總裁拉加德表示曾討論立即升息，
將等待更多數據，而法國公債風險溢酬升高亦可能限制政策操作空間。
企業獲利則是 10 月歐股表現的重要支撐 。 依LSEG 截至 10 月 29 日彙整的 144 家已公布財報企
業及其餘公司預估，STOXX 600 第三季獲利年增率上修至 23.6%，接近第二季 23.9%的成長幅度，
營收預估年增 13.6%。能源類股受油價上揚與煉油利差改善帶動，獲利預估年增139%，為整體成長
主要來源，且明顯高於其他產業；扣除能源後，其餘企業獲利成長約 9.8%，仍高於過去 16 季平均

7.1%。產業分化持續明顯，國防與部分科技設備商受惠訂單需求，ASML 與 Ericsson 公布的業績亦
反映設備支出韌性；反觀汽車、零售等非必需消費產業面臨需求疲弱，房地產業受高利率與融資成本
壓抑，市場對財測指引及現金流品質的要求提高。
展望 11 月，歐洲能源股短期受惠高油價及煉油利差，但獲利基期與地緣政治變動可能擴大股價
波動，宜避免將當季高成長率直接推算為全年可望維持相同成長動能；國防、電力設備及具全球競爭
力的工業企業，若訂單與定價能力維持，仍具相對優勢。金融股須區分銀行個別資本體質與法國主權
財政風險 ， 並觀察公債利差變化對融資成本的傳導 ； 房地產及高度依賴歐洲內需的循環性消費產業則
偏審慎。後續關注 ECB 十二月利率決策、能源通膨走勢、法國預算協商及企業財測修正，若殖利率
再度上升而獲利改善未能擴散，歐股評價上行空間將受限制，個股分化可能延續。

日本股市：日銀暫緩升息但確認物價達標，半導體回檔後聚焦財報與 12 月政策
10 月日本股市的觀察主軸，集中於日本銀行政策動向、通膨變化與 AI 投資帶動的出口成長。日
銀於 10 月 30 日維持政策利率 1.25%不變，惟投票比由 1 票支持升息、其餘委員主張維持利率，轉
為 5 比 4，顯示內部對進一步緊縮的支持增加。會後市場對升息時點的預期並未明顯改變，焦點仍集
中於 12 月。日銀展望報告指出，通膨大致符合 2%目標，並將 2027 財年核心 CPI 預估由 2.4%上修
至 2.6%。此外，ETF 年出售規模由 3,300 億日圓調降至 2,860 億日圓，反映政策正常化過程仍兼顧

市場承受能力，整體呈現利率暫停調整、緊縮基調延續的格局。
基本面方面，日本 9 月出口年增 16.7%，雖低於 8 月的 19.3%，仍連續第 13 個月成長，顯示外
需維持韌性 。 半導體等電子零組件 、 汽車及半導體製造設備出口 ， 分別成長49.4%、15.1%及 43.9%，
其中 AI 相關需求成為重要支柱。依出口地區觀察，對美出口年增 22.9%，半導體製造設備更大增
93.1%；對亞洲出口成長 17.5%，亦提供主要支撐。相較之下，對中國出口增幅放緩至 8.6%，主因
汽車出口年減 59.8%， 顯示不同市場與產業的表現仍有落差 。 整體而言 ， 出口成長並非全面同步擴張 ，
而是由 AI 投資與半導體設備需求帶動，後續仍須觀察成長動能能否延伸至更多產業。
展望 11 月，日本股市可聚焦通膨走勢、日銀政策訊號，以及AI 資本支出的延續性。9 月 CPI 年
增率由 1.9%升至 2.1%，若加回能源政策約 0.82 個百分點的壓抑效果，實質漲幅達2.9%，加上食品
與外食價格仍有上漲壓力，支持日銀維持偏緊縮立場。若後續數據持續反映物價壓力，12 月升息預

期可能成為影響市場評價的重要因素。產業方面，全球 AI 基礎建設與半導體資本支出若持續擴張，
日本半導體設備及電子零組件出口可望獲得支撐；韓國 10 月上旬半導體出口大幅成長，也提供區域
需求的觀察線索。不過，對中出口放緩及汽車需求變化，仍是評估外需時不可忽略的風險。
整體而言，AI 投資熱潮與出口成長韌性，為日本股市後續表現帶來正向契機。儘管日銀政策正常
化仍是市場關注焦點，但全球 AI 基礎建設持續推進，有望支撐日本半導體設備及電子零組件需求，
並為相關企業帶來營運成長機會 。 可持續聚焦出口擴張與科技投資所帶動的基本面改善 。 若需求動能
延續並轉化為獲利成長，日本股市可望在政策調整過程中，持續展現結構性成長題材的吸引力。

台灣股市：外銷訂單連四月創高，台積電上修展望，市場料更重視獲利驗證
10 月台股基本面持續交出亮眼成績 。 經濟部公布 ，9 月外銷訂單達 1,142.1 億美元 ， 月增10.9%、
年增 62.4%，連續第四個月創單月新高，且已連續兩個月突破千億美元；第三季外銷訂單3,151.0 億
美元，年增 65.0%，累計前三季 8,192 億美元，已超越去年全年的 7,437 億美元。貨品別方面，資
訊通信與電子產品分別達 438.8 億及 474.2 億美元，年增 87.3%及 71.0%，皆創單月新高；地區別
方面，美國訂單 453.8 億美元、年增 79.4%，主要來自伺服器、手機及筆電。企業端，台積電第三

季營收 467.2 億美元，季增 16.2%並優於財測高標，第四季營收預估 523 億至 543 億美元，全年美
元營收成長上修至約 44%，顯示 AI 需求仍未降溫。
對照廠商對 9 月接單的預期與實際結果，可發現 AI 需求向供應鏈擴散的判斷大致獲得驗證。當
時資訊通信與電子產品的訂單金額動向指數分別為 52.4 及 51.5，實際接單即雙雙創下單月新高；基
本金屬與化學品的動向指數高達 59.0 及 61.0，9 月接單亦分別年增 29.7%及 22.8%，其中基本金屬
受惠 AI 供應鏈帶動銅箔、銅箔基板需求增加。機械指數雖僅 46.3，但在半導體業者擴大資本支出帶
動下，9 月接單仍年增 21.5%並創同月新高。與 AI 供應鏈相關的傳統貨品表現較佳，但部分傳產仍
面臨供給過剩壓力，產業並非全面同步復甦。
展望 11 月，台股將在強勁基本面與外部評價調整之間拉鋸。10 月南韓股市因記憶體競爭與 AI
投資疑慮重挫 ， 台股相對抗跌 ， 加權指數月中一度再創歷史新高 ， 但10/15 及 10/30 仍分別出現 3.41%

與 2.49%的單日跌幅，顯示外資對 AI 題材的獲利了結賣壓仍在。經濟部預估 10 月外銷訂單將介於
1,125 億至 1,159 億美元 ， 年增62%至 67%， 按金額計算的動向指數為50.5，若高於 9 月的 1,142.1
億美元，將連續第五個月創單月新高。台積電亦重申 2026 年資本支出 600 億至 640 億美元，並表
示 2026 至 2028 年支出將顯著高於前三年 ， 設備 、 廠務及先進封裝供應鏈的訂單能見度仍高 。 不過，
台積電也提醒消費性與價格敏感市場正面臨零組件漲價與總經不確定性 ， 對2027 年規劃採審慎態度 。
整體而言，11 月維持偏正面的台股觀點，以重視獲利驗證作為配置方向。核心部位可聚焦晶圓
代工、先進封裝、伺服器代工及 ASIC 等需求明確且具定價能力的企業；擴散機會則可從銅箔基板、
高階材料 、 設備與廠務工程中 ， 篩選已取得實際訂單 、 營收與毛利率同步成長的公司 。 對於評價已高、
主要依賴題材想像的個股，宜適度觀察，避免在市場重新檢視 AI 投入回收時承受較大波動。留意 11

月中下旬公布的 10 月外銷訂單、美國大型科技業者資本支出指引及輝達財報對市場情緒的影響。

中國股市：外需撐起生產，內需與房市仍疲弱，政策加碼成為關鍵
中國第三季經濟表現略優於預期 。 國家統計局公布 ， 第三季GDP 年增 4.5%， 高於第二季的4.3%，
前三季累計成長 4.6%，仍落在政府全年 4.5%至 5.0%的目標區間內。成長動能主要來自供給端與外
需：第三季製造業增加值年增 5.9%，資訊傳輸、軟體與資訊技術服務業年增9.8%；前三季貨物進出
口年增 18.8%，受惠全球 AI 開發加速與電動車需求擴大，高科技產品出口表現強勁。另一方面，受
中東情勢推升原油與原物料價格影響，9 月 PPI 年增 5.0%，為今年以來最大漲幅，上游資源類企業

的獲利有望改善，但中下游製造業則面臨成本轉嫁壓力。
然而，從圖中可見，生產面與需求面的落差仍大。9 月工業生產年增 5.5%，服務業生產指數年
增 4.8%，兩者維持溫和成長；但房地產仍是經濟最大拖累，9 月房地產投資年減 26.2%，跌幅較 8
月的 25.4%進一步擴大。銷售端同步轉弱，銷售面積與銷售額分別年減 20.5%及 20.0%，較 8 月的
14.7%及 12.1%明顯惡化；竣工面積跌幅更由 28.4%擴大至 36.4%。新開工跌幅雖由 31.0%收斂至
25.6%，在建面積則持平於年減 12.8%，顯示開發商仍以去化庫存、控制投資為主，房市尚未出現止
穩訊號，並持續壓抑家庭財富效果與地方政府的土地財政收入。
內需疲弱是當前中國經濟的核心課題。9 月社會消費品零售總額僅年增 1.1%，汽車與家電等耐
用品銷售停滯，以舊換新補貼效果逐漸遞減；前三季固定資產投資年減 7.2%，基建與工業投資同步
下滑，第三季建築業增加值亦年減 4.4%。統計局也坦言國內需求不足的問題仍然突出。政策方面，7

月中央政治局會議定調下半年實施更積極的財政政策與適度寬鬆的貨幣政策 ， 並適時推出增量政策；
10 月下旬召開的二十屆五中全會則以黨建議題為主。市場表現上，10/30 上證綜合指數受半導體股
拖累下跌 1.44%，香港恒生指數則在必需消費與能源股帶動下上漲 1.46%，資金明顯偏好防禦型內
需與高股息族群。
整體而言，對中國股市宜維持中性偏審慎的態度。經濟成長雖守住目標區間，但房地產投資與銷
售跌幅擴大、零售成長放緩，內需尚未出現明確回升訊號，在增量刺激政策落地前，指數大幅走高的
條件仍不充分。配置上可聚焦兩條主線：一是受惠出口與產業升級的高端製造、資訊科技服務與國產
算力供應鏈，但須留意資本支出擴張對現金流的壓力；二是具穩定配息與現金流的高股息、公用事業
及必需消費族群 ， 在內需疲弱時提供防禦 。 後續可觀察年底中央經濟工作會議對財政擴張與房市支持
的力度，作為調整布局的依據。

拉美市場：魯拉意外連任引發財政紀律疑慮，巴西股市重挫
10 月拉美市場走勢主要受巴西總統大選左右。月初弗拉維奧・波索納洛意外在首輪總統選舉中
領先，提高市場對政黨輪替與財政改革的期待，帶動巴西股市同步走強；但 10 月 25 日現任總統
魯拉在決選中仍以 51.79%的得票率勝出，市場原先的期待落空，轉而擔憂巴西財政赤字可能進一步
惡化，巴西資產因此遭到拋售。由於巴西約占 MSCI 拉丁美洲指數約六成權重，巴西股市下跌使拉
美市場整體出現明顯回落，後續巴西股市能否回穩，仍要觀察魯拉新任期提出的具體財政政策。
市場之所以對魯拉的財政政策如此敏感，主要是因為巴西的債務負擔仍在持續增加。高盛預估，

巴西一般政府總債務占國內生產毛額的比率 ， 將由 2025 年的 78.6%升至 2026 年底的 83.5%。然
而，巴西部分支出不易削減，既有稅負又偏高，使政府調整收支的空間相當有限。在此情況下，高盛
預期魯拉新任期可能僅能溫和抑制不含利息的政府支出成長 ， 並將依賴增加稅收來改善收支 ， 推動大
幅結構性改革的可能性則相對較低。若巴西財政調整最終以增稅為主，而支出未能有效控制，市場不
僅會擔心巴西財政赤字難以縮小，也會擔心新增稅額侵蝕企業獲利。
此外，即使魯拉政府提出新的財政改革方案，仍可能受制於政治立場相左的新任國會。在本次國
會改選後，右派與中間偏右政黨合計將掌握眾議院約 59%、參議院約 64%的席次，弗拉維奧所屬的
自由黨也成為參眾兩院的最大黨，因此魯拉推動預算與改革時，必須爭取政治立場不同的議員支持。
高盛報告指出，儘管巴西國會中間政黨仍可能與魯拉政府合作，但可能以內閣職位、預算分配及國營
企業人事作為交換條件 。 這樣的協商雖有助魯拉政府在國會取得多數支持 ， 卻也可能使原有方案遭到

修改，進而影響巴西財政整頓的幅度與速度。
巴西財政政策的變化，最終會透過利率與匯率影響股市。巴西現行 Selic 政策利率為 13.75%，
選前市場原本預期巴西央行將延續降息步調，但選後巴西里爾急貶，進口成本上升恐進一步推升通
膨，限制巴西央行降息空間，在利率條件緊縮的狀況下，也將使仰賴國內需求的企業獲利受到壓抑。
不過，儘管巴西財政政策仍有變數，巴西經濟本身仍具備一定的支撐條件。高盛報告指出，巴西
農業具國際競爭力，油氣產量持續成長，並在綠色能源與關鍵礦物領域具有發展潛力；此外，巴西的
貿易順差 、 外國直接投資與充裕外匯存底 ， 也能為外部資金波動提供緩衝 ， 持續為巴西經濟提供支撐 。
整體而言，巴西的產業優勢與外部收支條件，使巴西經濟基本面仍具支撐。然而，魯拉新任期的
財政部長人選、2027 年預算安排，以及魯拉政府與國會的協商進展，將左右市場對巴西財政與降息
的預期，在財政政策方向明朗之前，短線拉美市場波動恐將加劇。

科技類股：CSP 資本支出續增壓縮現金流，AI 受惠族群走向分化
10 月下旬美國大型科技業者第三季財報，讓市場對 AI 的關注由投入多少轉向何時回收。營運面
普遍不差：Meta 第三季營收 646 億美元、年增 26%，廣告單價年增 14%，顯示 AI 精準投放已轉化
為實際收入；Alphabet 旗下 Google Cloud 營收年增 103%至 307.1 億美元。然而，Alphabet 搜尋
營收成長放緩至 12%，加上自由現金流為負 90.7 億美元、遠差於市場預期，股價於 10/29 單日重挫
12.6%，創上市以來最大單日跌幅；Meta 自由現金流亦轉為負 21.2 億美元。相較之下，微軟資本支

出低於預期、自由現金流維持正值。市場並非否定 AI 需求，而是開始以現金流紀律區分企業。
從四大雲端服務業者 （CSP） 最新一季的營業現金流與資本支出 ， 可清楚看出壓力所在 。Amazon
資本支出約 759 億美元，遠高於約424 億美元的營業現金流，缺口最大；Alphabet 資本支出約 514
億美元，同樣超過約 423 億美元的營業現金流；Meta 資本支出約 349 億美元，亦略高於約 328 億
美元的營業現金流。四家業者中，僅微軟營業現金流約 580 億美元，明顯高於約 394 億美元的資本
支出。這意味多數 CSP 已需動用既有現金或對外融資支應 AI 建置，投資節奏將更受資金成本與股東
回報要求牽制 ； 但從另一面看 ， 各業者全年資本支出指引並未下修 ，Meta 與 Alphabet 分別維持 1,350
億至 1,450 億美元及 1,950 億至 2,050 億美元的區間，對上游供應鏈而言，短期訂單能見度仍高。
依 AI 需求能見度與財務韌性兩個面向劃分科技子產業版圖來看。核心受惠區的大型 CSP、半導

體設備、CPU／ASIC 及高階光通訊，兼具明確訂單與穩健財務，例如Lumentum 最新一季營收年增
109%、非 GAAP 毛利率突破 50%，並指出部分雷射元件供給仍吃緊。穩健型的消費電子、手機晶
片與類比 IC，AI 需求能見度較低，但財務結構穩定，可在科技股波動時發揮防禦作用；伺服器整機
與組裝雖受惠出貨成長，卻面臨毛利偏薄、零組件成本與能源成本上升的風險，股價波動較大。至於
高槓桿的 AI 算力出租業者，在利率居高與信用利差擴大下最為脆弱，10 月 CoreWeave 債券價格下
跌即引發市場對 AI 信用風險的擔憂，成為本波修正的導火線之一。
整體而言，科技股以「核心基本面受惠為主、穩健型為輔」來觀察。可聚焦半導體設備、CPU
／ASIC 及高階光通訊等兼具訂單能見度與財務韌性的族群，並以 11 月指標性廠商 Lumentum、輝
達等財報驗證需求延續性；大型 CSP 中，則可優先選擇營業現金流足以支應資本支出、投資節奏較
具紀律的業者。穩健型的消費電子與手機晶片等，可作為降低整體波動的衛星配置。伺服器整機與組

裝族群宜觀察具議價能力者，避免在零組件與能源成本上升時承受毛利壓力。AI 長期趨勢未變，但
市場已進入用現金流與業績說話的階段 ， 選股應更重視需求能否轉化為獲利， 對科技股維持正向看法。

能源類股：柴油短缺支撐油價，但若中東衝突緩解，明年原油有望轉為供過於求
10 月西德州原油價格隨荷莫茲海峽油輪與沙烏地阿拉伯能源設施持續遇襲，整體走勢仍呈現震
盪走高。展望 11 月，偏低的能源庫存與冬季需求預期仍有利油價支撐；然而，國際能源署（IEA）、
美國能源資訊署（EIA）與石油輸出國組織（OPEC）等 3 大能源機構於 10 月月報同步下修今年石
油需求預估，且 IEA 與 EIA 均預期明年供給增幅將遠高於需求，故若後續中東局勢出現好轉，石油
供應緊張情況也將有望逐步緩解。
3 大能源機構在 10 月月報均下修今年需求預估，IEA 將 2026 年全球石油需求由年減每日

250 萬桶調整為年減 260 萬桶，EIA 由年減約 170 萬桶調整為年減約 200 萬桶，OPEC 則由年
增每日 38 萬桶下修至 18 萬桶，預估分歧主要來自中國、中東等非 OECD 國家的需求預估。
展望明年，IEA 與 EIA 均預期石油市場將轉趨寬鬆。IEA 預估 2027 年全球供給將較今年增加
每日 770 萬桶 ， 需求僅增加 200 萬桶 ；EIA 則預估供給增加約每日 850 萬桶 ， 需求僅增加約 220
萬桶，顯示若後續中東受阻產能能夠恢復，供應回升速度將快於消費。OPEC 的預估則相對看好油
價，OPEC 將 2027 年需求由 9 月預估的每日增加 236 萬桶上調至增加 255 萬桶，並預估市場
需要 OPEC+ 提供的原油將由今年的每日 4,210 萬桶至明年增至 4,390 萬桶，換言之，OPEC 認
為明年需求成長將超過其他產油國的供給增幅，OPEC+須再增產量才能滿足市場需求。
然而，即使中東衝突後續轉趨緩解，明年原油供需可能轉趨寬鬆，成品油供應仍可能維持緊俏。

IEA 指出，9 月全球柴油出口較去年同期減少約每日 70 萬桶，主因是中東成品油運輸受阻與俄羅
斯煉油設施受損，加上秋季煉油廠進入歲修，使市場難以在入冬前補足柴油庫存。因此，IEA 認為即
使 2027 年第一季全球石油市場恢復供過於求，較明顯的庫存回補仍可能要等到明年下半年。由於
成品油供應偏緊，EIA 雖然下修石油需求預測，卻仍將 2026 年西德州原油均價預測由每桶 84.65
美元上調至 88.21 美元，2027 年則由 69.74 美元上調至 79.74 美元。
另一方面，各國政策措施可望在過渡期間持續提供供應緩衝。G7 於 10 月 2 日承諾在四個月
內協調釋出 1 億桶原油與油品，並優先供應柴油；美國放寬俄羅斯柴油交易限制後，美聯社引述船
運追蹤機構 Kpler 指出，首艘載運約 4 萬噸柴油的油輪已經啟航，預計 11 月 7 日抵達紐約，均
有利於緩解短期成品油短缺問題。整體而言，偏低的成品油庫存與中東航運風險，短期仍有利支撐油
價，然而在三大機構均下修今年石油需求預估 ， 且IEA 與 EIA 預期明年供給增幅將明顯高於需求的情

況下，後續油價進一步大幅上漲空間也相對有限。

黃金類股：中國及全球央行買盤仍穩健，然短線市場資金轉向流出
近期金價走勢仍未擺脫偏弱格局 ， 主因是中東能源供應風險推升油價， 市場擔憂通膨難以迅速降
溫，並持續評估聯準會於今明兩年再度升息，而利率維持高檔將提高持有黃金的機會成本。此外，
10 月中下旬開始部分避險資金轉向美國公債，北美黃金 ETF 也出現資金撤出，使金價即使在美元
與美債殖利率出現回落的情況下，仍缺乏買盤支撐。然而， 中長線來看，全球央行購金的趨勢目前
仍未出現明顯轉向，加上中國投資買盤持續流入，均有助支撐中長期黃金需求。
從黃金 ETF 資金來看，黃金投資需求在 10 月下旬由全面流入轉為地區分化。世界黃金協會資

料顯示，9 月全球實物黃金 ETF 淨流入 100 億美元，持有量增加 67 噸至 4,256 噸，創下持有
量歷史新高；第三季累計淨流入 310 億美元，也創下單季新高紀錄，然而，截至 10 月 23 日的
一週，全球黃金 ETF 轉為淨流出約 12 億美元，持有量減少  7.7 噸，其中北美黃金 ETF 淨流出
16.9 億美元，顯示隨金價再度向下破底，ETF 資金也開始轉趨保守。
然而另一方面，中國投資需求仍持續為金價提供支撐。世界黃金協會指出，中國黃金 ETF 9 月
淨流入人民幣 110 億元，持有量增加 11 噸至 304 噸，創下月底新高，且截至 10 月 23 日買
盤仍在延續。世界黃金協會指出中國股市表現偏弱，提高了當地投資人分散資產的需求，而中國公
債殖利率下降，也降低了持有黃金的機會成本，雙雙有利於中國資金流入黃金市場。
此外，央行購金仍是金價的結構性支撐。世界黃金協會統計，全球央行 8 月申報的淨購金量為
39 噸；另中國人民銀行 9 月增持 23 噸，為其連續第 23 個月增持黃金配置，波蘭央行年初至 8

月也累計購入 98 噸 ， 使其總持有量達 648 噸 ， 逐步接近波蘭央行設下的 700 噸目標 。 另一方面，
由於許多央行購金並未公開申報，高盛以倫敦金庫黃金流出量與英國黃金淨出口之間的差額，推估
未申報的央行買盤，估計全球央行 8 月實際購金量約 79 噸，經季節調整的三個月平均約每月 85
噸，遠高於 2022 年以前平均每月約 17 噸的水準。高盛報告指出，新興市場央行的黃金配置比重
仍低於長期合理水準，加上各國仍有動機分散持有七大工業國（G7）貨幣資產，因此預估在未來央
行買盤仍可望延續。
但仍需注意的是， 也有個別央行開始調整黃金配置 。 哈薩克央行於 10 月中旬表示 ， 由於 10 年
期美債殖利率升至 24 年高點，提高了美元資產的吸引力，故計畫在 2026 年底前，將黃金占儲備
資產的比重由約 78% 降至 75%。以哈薩克央行 386 噸的黃金持有量估算，此次調整規模約相當於
15 噸。雖然哈薩克央行的調整規模不大，但若其他黃金占比偏高的央行也跟進再平衡，全球央行需

求對金價的支撐力道可能因此減弱。
整體而言，全球央行購金趨勢尚未出現明顯轉向，加上中國投資買盤，均有助支撐中長期黃金需
求。然而，現階段高漲的通膨與升息預期，加上短線 ETF 資金撤出，仍可能使短期金價面臨震盪。

固定收益：避險需求支撐美債，聚焦中期美債及投資級債
10 月美國公債殖利率呈現月初攀升、月中震盪回落、月底再度下行的走勢。月初受能源價格上
漲、通膨預期升溫，以及 AI 投資帶動經濟成長與資金需求增加影響，10 年期美債殖利率於 10 月 5
日升至 5.31%，30 年期升至 5.66%。隨後市場對 AI 基礎建設融資與企業現金流的疑慮升高，科技股
賣壓帶動資金轉向公債，10 年期殖利率於 10 月 15 日降至 5.10%；22 日大型科技股重挫再度推升
避險需求，進一步降至 4.96%。月底雖然美國經濟維持韌性、聯準會於 28 日維持利率不變，通膨及

財政赤字仍限制長天期利率下行空間，但科技企業資本支出疑慮持續支撐債市。
美國經濟呈現就業成長放緩 、 通膨壓力仍高的局面 。9 月非農就業僅增加 2.9 萬人 ， 失業率由4.1%
升至 4.2%，反映企業聘僱動能趨緩；惟通膨尚未明顯改善，9 月 CPI 年增 3.7%，月增 0.6%，主要
受能源價格上漲帶動，核心CPI 年增 2.6%。聯準會偏好的核心 PCE 物價指數年增 3.1%，仍高於 2%
目標。Fed 於 10 月 28 日以 10 比 2 票維持政策利率於 3.75%至 4.00%不變，並將經濟活動評估由
「穩健」上調至「強勁」 ，指出消費支出具韌性、企業資本投資維持強勁，兩位官員則因通膨疑慮主
張升息一碼。Fed 強調將等待更多經濟數據再決定政策方向，未明確承諾後續升息時點，顯示就業放
緩與通膨黏著仍是政策判斷的主要拉鋸因素。
信用市場方面，十月中旬CoreWeave 公司債價格重挫，凸顯人工智慧基礎建設高度仰賴外部融
資的脆弱環節。根據路透報導，公司債務規模約五百億美元，部分債券殖利率一度攀升至百分之十五

至十六，五年期信用違約交換利差突破一千一百個基點，顯示投資人要求的違約風險補償大幅提高。
疑慮集中於建置成本、設備折舊、客戶收款與債務支出的時間差，以及再融資成本。即使需求強勁，
合約收入仍不等同即時現金流 ， 建置延宕可能加重償債壓力 。 相關賣壓亦使投資人重新區分大型科技
企業與高度槓桿資料中心營運商的信用體質。此事件尚不代表整體投資級信用惡化。
展望十一月 ， 美債配置建議仍以中期公債與優質美元投資級公司債為核心 ， 兼顧較高票息水準與
政策預期反覆下的存續期間風險 。 十年期殖利率回落至百分之五附近後 ， 中天期債券仍較超長天期不
易受期限溢酬波動干擾 。 投資級公司債可優先篩選利息保障倍數充足 、 現金流穩健及再融資需求可控
的發行人，避免僅憑信用評等忽略產業資本支出周期。高收益債須檢視利差補償，尤其資料中心借款
人的抵押品與客戶集中度。後續關注十一月美國期中選舉、就業與通膨數據、財政部發債規畫及聯準
會十二月會議預期，並追蹤信用利差是否由個別高槓桿企業擴散至整體市場。

研究員/莊佳蓉 EVA8894122@uni-psg.com

研究部維持台達電買進投資建議，主係：(1) 3Q26受缺料影響出貨
遞延，並認列存貨跌價損失，但漲價效益將自4Q26起逐步反映，延

心相關營收占比已達五成，液冷今年占比約13%，全年營收可望突
破900億元，明年仍維持成長；(3) 缺電與缺料雖然影響資料中心
建置節奏，但管理層表示訂單能見度仍高，主要是出貨時點遞延，
需求並未消失；(4) 股價回檔幅度已大，評價已明顯回落。綜上所
述，研究部微幅下修27年營收1.04兆元(原估1.05兆元)，EPS下修
至60.37元(原估62.68元)。評價方面，考量HVDC滲透率仍不明朗，
僅給予31倍本益比，目標價由2,400元下修至1,870元。整體而言，
營運最壞情況已過且股價回檔已多，維持買進建議。

重點摘要： 
1. 3Q26受缺料干擾，毛利率承壓但OPM大致持穩 
3Q26營收1,971億元(原估2,102億元)， 季增7.5%， 年增31.1%，
營收達成率93.7%，低於預期，主因是資料中心客戶長短料、
建置延遲，造成出貨遞延。毛利率降至34.1%(原估35.4%)，季
減1.6pts， 低於預期 ， 主係因出貨遞延公司因此認列約39億元
存貨跌價損失(約影響1.7%，涉及產品非VR相關產品，研究部
推測為ASIC產品)，加上漲價在3Q26幾乎還沒反映。不過費用
控管得宜，營利率16.6%(原估16.9%)，僅季減0.1pts，本業獲
利能力大致持穩 。 業外收益受股市評價影響 ， 由2Q26的44億元
降至10億元，EPS為9.04元(原估10.54元)，低於預期。

2. 4Q26毛利率可望止跌回升，營運低點已過 
由於缺料尚未完全解除，研究部微幅調整4Q26營收至2,248億
元(原估2,253億元)，但季增14.1%、年增39.1%，成長動能仍
強。獲利方面，公司表示4Q毛利率優於3Q，主係存貨跌價損失
新增認列有望減少 、 開始有較多新價格體現， 以及電源業務成
長 帶 動 產 品 組 合 改 善 ， 故 研 究 部 預 估 毛 利 率34.8%( 原估
35.8%)， 季增0.7pts； 營利率17.2%(原估18.2%)， 季增0.6pts；
EPS下修至10.14元(原估11.03元)。展望1Q27，研究部預估營
收2,308億元，季增2.6%，表現優於以往的淡季季節性，主要
是部分AI遞延訂單可望延續至1Q27出貨。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

3. 資料中心營收占比過半，訂單能見度仍高 
管理層表示 ， 今年資料中心相關營收已占總營收一半 ，冷卻系
統約占13%，液冷3Q營收約250億元，26年營收可望突破900億
元 ， 且公司對1H27液冷需求看法仍偏樂觀 。雖然缺電影響部分
資料中心的建置進度 ，但管理層強調訂單能見度高， 變化主要
是出貨時點的延後，並非需求消失。此外，研究部認為，台達
電在供應鏈管理上具備優勢， 隨著缺料逐漸緩解 ，遞延的需求
可望陸續轉為營收。

4. 然Rubin Ultra機櫃出現低壓方案，HVDC滲透率仍待觀察 
HVDC方面 ， 正負400V進展較快 ， 已開始出貨 ；800V則由NV主推 ，
進度較慢。根據供應鏈調查，Ultra平台目前已有低壓設計方
案，若TDP增幅不大，HVDC的省電效益相對有限，客戶未必非
用800V不可，因此研究部下修2027年營收預估。此外，公司已
通過馬來西亞檳城新據點的投資案 ， 為中長期AI電源需求預先
擴充產能。在Vicor相關訴訟上，仍在ITC調查程序中，短期對
出貨不會有影響，公司對自身IP技術具有信心。

5. 評價已回落，維持買進 
綜上所述，研究部下修2026年營收至7,645億元(原估7,781億
元)，年增37.8%；毛利率35.3%(原估35.9%)；EPS下修至36.77
元(原估39.16元)。 同步下修2027年營收至1.04兆元(原估1.05
兆元)， 年增36.4%； 毛利率36.1%(原估36.5%)；EPS下修至60.37
元(原估62.68元)，年增64.2%。考量HVDC滲透率仍不明朗，僅
給予31倍本益比， 目標價由2,400元下修至1,870元 。 惟股價評
價已明顯回落，且營運最壞情況已過，維持買進建議。

評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

### Page 3

Hon Hai (2317.TW; Buy, on CL): New AI server racks and Foldable phones to drive 4Q26E

We expect Hon Hai’s monthly revenues to continue sequential growth through Dec 
2026, leading 4Q26 revenues to grow at +39% QoQ, driven by: (1) AI data center: The 
next-generation AI server racks and CPO switches ramp-up, with higher ASP and early 
delivery. We expect Hon Hai to be the first to deliver the latest model, around 2-3 
months ahead of peers, driving its Oct and onward revenue growth. Followed by 
GPU-powered AI server racks, the LPU-powered AI server racks should fuel the growth 
in Dec and onward, supporting the YoY increase in 1Q27. As our Global Server TAM 
update shows, we expect the AI server rack global value TAM to grow at 163% CAGR in 
2025-28E to US$1tn, or 63% of the global server value TAM by 2028E. And (2) 
Smartphone: riding on the major brand customer’s latest model launches along with the 
new form factor to attract more end users. As we highlighted in September (report link), 
we reduced our global smartphone volume estimates by 1m/ 8m/ 0.2m units to 1.1bn / 
1.2bn/ 1.2bn in 2026-28E, or -10% / +2%/ +2% YoY on high memory costs; however, we 
remain positive on the potential for global leading brands to outperform the market 
given their strong brand awareness, large procurement power, and customers’ stronger 
consumption power. We remain positive on foldable phones and expect premium 
models to continue to gain market share, which should be positive to Hon Hai. Maintain 
Buy (on CL).

3-month revenues preview: We expect the company’s 4Q26E revenues to be +61% 
YoY/ +39% QoQ to NT$4.2 trillion. 3Q26 delivered strong revenues YoY growth of 47%, 
or 5% / 7% ahead of our / Bloomberg consensus, and we expect to see both YoY and 
MoM growth throughout Oct-Dec, driven by new AI server racks, new smartphone 
models, and CPO switches. Management noted that AI-related products remain on track 
to maintain solid growth momentum through 4Q26, while ICT products continue to 
benefit from typical 2H seasonal tailwinds, which should support overall operational 
performance in line with current consensus expectations.

### Page 4

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on an 17.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design, 
software, and semis; (3) slower-than-expected ramp-up of capacity globally; and (4) 
fiercer-than-expected competition in the consumer electronics EMS business.  
Quanta (2382.TW, Neutral): AI servers ramp-up to drive growth ahead; model transition and PC 
shipment slowdown could weigh on 4Q26

Quanta Sep revenues declined by 23% MoM, which we attributed to AI servers model 
transition, while PC entered quarter-end at a strong MoM of +62% MoM. Looking 
forward, we expect monthly revenues to resume sequential growth in Oct to Dec, riding 
on a reducing impact of AI servers model transition as new racks ramp up, along with 
growing general servers on agentic AI, and ASP increase on better specification and high 
raw material costs. We expect 4Q26E revenues to decline by 4% QoQ, considering the 
high base in 3Q26 and the gradual ramp-up of new AI server racks. As we highlighted in 
Oct (Global Server TAM updates), we expect global AI server racks (powered by NVIDIA 
and AMD) shipment at 63k / 110k / 164k in 2026E-28E along with the specification 
upgrade to drive the ASP; we raised leading US CSPs aggregate capex by 4% / 17% / 
22% in 2026E-28E to US$1,397bn by 2028E, reflecting our positive view on AI spending.

Maintain Neutral on fair valuation.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Quanta 
We are Neutral rated on Quanta. Our 12-month target price of NT$300 is based on a 
10.7x 2027E P/E, which references PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple 
correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance. Key 
upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery; 2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers. 
Wiwynn (6669.TW, Buy): New ASIC and GPU AI server racks in expansion

Wiwynn delivered strong Sep revenues, which were 19% ahead of our estimates, 
reflecting strong general server demand from US leading CSPs, and its major customers’ 
new ASIC AI server ramping up. We expect revenue YoY growth to remain strong at mid 
double-digit through Dec 2026, leading 4Q26 revenues to grow at +60 YoY / +8% QoQ, 
driven by (1) major customers’ new ASIC AI servers ramping up, across air-cooled and 
liquid-cooled, (2) general servers end demand remaining solid on agentic AI, (3) new 
GPU-powered AI server racks entering mass production in 4Q26 across NVIDIA and 
AMD platforms, and (4) potential new customers in ASIC AI servers. As we highlighted in 
Oct (Global Server TAM update), we raised AI servers implied AI chips shipment by 9% / 
19% in 2027 / 28E with ASIC penetration rate increasing to 58% by 2028E, and our US 
internet team forecasts that leading US cloud service providers (CSPs) in aggregate will 
grow their capex budgets by 84%/ 52%/ 13% YoY in 2026-28E, with our US CSP capex 
estimates raised by 4% / 17% / 22% in 2026-28E to US$1,397bn by 2028E, supporting 
our positive view on Wiwynn. Maintain Buy with an unchanged 12m TP of NT$4,000

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wiwynn 
We are Buy-rated on Wiwynn with a 12-month TP of NT$4,000. Our target price is 
based on 20.3x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance. 
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected AI server demand growth; 2) 
slower-than-expected recovery in general server demand; and 3) more severe 
competition in the server ODM market.  
Wistron (3231.TW, Buy): AI server racks to see strong ramp-up with customer expansion and 
specification upgrades

Wistron’s Sep revenue was up 87% YoY to NT$381bn, or 18% lower than our estimate, 
which we attribute to the impact of shipment timing volatility in higher-ASP AI products. 
We expect sequential revenue growth ahead through Dec 2026, driven by new GPU AI 
server racks ramp-up in 4Q26, which should drive up the ASP , along with strong AI 
spending and expansion of the company’s customer base. The new GPU AI server racks 
have various racks across GPU, CPU, and LPU, supporting ODM growth in coming 
quarters. Sep PC shipment was up 7% MoM but down 32% YoY to 1.5m units, reflecting 
the slow PC demand amid the consumption pull-in under the high memory cost. Despite 
PC shipments growth likely being negative HoH considering the strong pull-in since late 
2025, we expect the strong AI server rack ramp-up to offset the impact, driving 2H26 
revenue up +41% HoH. Maintain Buy with an unchanged TP of NT$291 (12.6x 2027E P/E 
vs. 30.4% NI YoY in 2027-28E).

ASUS (2357.TW, Neutral): AI server racks in expansion along with PC product mix upgrade

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Inventec  
We are Neutral-rated on Inventec with a 12-month target price of NT$61.0. Our target 
price is based on 14.2x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth 
vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s 
performance. 
Key upside/downside risks: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

### Page 14

(2) broadband, IoT, and game console entering peak season. We are positive on

Maintain Sell on stretched valuation.

Pegatron’s Sep revenue was up 87% YoY / 143% MoM to NT$215bn, or 64% higher than 
our estimate. This reflects a strong ramp-up in AI servers in Sep. Sep PC shipments were 
up c.8% MoM but down 44% YoY to 0.46m, reflecting the weak PC demand under the 
high memory costs. Prior to September, the company suffered from model transition (i.e. 
declining demand for older models while new models were in a slow shipment ramp-up 
phase).

Earnings revision: We factor in Pegatron’s Sep revenue and raise our 2026-28E net 
income by 4% / 6% / 4%, mainly on higher revenues and lower opex ratio. We raise our 
2026-28E revenues by 16% / 28% / 27%, considering the AI server shipment ramp-up 
and higher ASP on better specification. Given the much lower GM of AI server racks 
compared to Pegatron’s other products, our changes in gross profits narrow to 9% / 12% 
/ 15% in 2026E-28E. The opex ratio for 2026-28E is cut by 0.1 ppt/ 0.2 ppt/ 0.1 ppt to 
reflect the improved operational efficiency.

on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 6

Realtek (2379.TW)
29 October 2026 Citi Research
6
Realtek
Company description
Founded in 1987, Realtek is a leading fabless semiconductor with
headquarters in Hsinchu, Taiwan. It has an extensive global footprint with
operational offices in China, Japan, Korea, Singapore, and the US. Realtek's
product portfolio is well-diversified; generally, it can be categorized into three
business groups: Communications Network (Wi-Fi, Bluetooth, automotive
Ethernet, PC Ethernet, switch, etc.), Computer Peripherals (audio codec,

clock IC, card reader, IP cam SoC, USB  hub controller, Type-C controller, SSD
controller) and Multimedia (TV SoC, LCD controller, STB SoC, etc.).
Investment strategy
We rate the shares Buy. We believe Realtek is well-positioned to ride on
continued specs upgrade demand for WiFi, switch and 10G PON as well as the
strong advancements in the automotive and Io T sectors. In the long term, we
believe Realtek's cost-effective, high-performance, and comprehensive wired

and wireless solutions should become more critical with the growing
connectivity requirement at the edge.
Valuation
Our target price for Realtek is set at NT$900 based on our target PE multiple
of 20x to our 2027 EPS. We believe our PER of 20x, higher than its 3-year
forward PER average, is justified by its solid growth opportunity in automotive
and switch/PON business. We also believe its continued efforts in products
mix optimization should help to support its margins expansion. At our target

price, the shares would trade at a 2026/27E P/B of 8x/6x.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price include: 1) Slowdown in technology
migration for its networking business; 2) Weaker-than-expected growth
momentum for automotive Ethernet sales; 3) Worse-than-expected PC/NB
and consumer electronic demand; 4) Slower-than-expected GM expansion
due to pricing competition from peers; and 5) Higher-than-expected memory
cost burden to dampen the order outlook from its networking and PC/NB

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

filing. Management flagged shortages of components, power and skilled 
electricians and slower-than-expected data centre buildout to be potential 
hiccups in the process; however, it still sees strong underlying demand. As 
such, we still expect sequential revenue growth in both 4Q26E and 1Q27E 
as component shortages had deferred 3Q26 demand. On rising material 
costs, Delta noted that BoM costs have increased by 2% as of 3Q26 (vs. 
early 2026), with the company gradually passing these costs through to 
end customers in 4Q26 and 1Q27. We believe the main gross margin 
driver will likely still be the absence of inventory write-offs and a higher 
power mix in 4Q26E. We forecast a gross margin recovery to 36% and a 
record operating margin of 18.5% in 4Q26. As for long-term growth, we 
maintain our investment theses that DC-DC module and HVDC adoption 
will become key growth drivers for Delta in 2027 and 2028, respectively.

3Q26 results missed on gross margin. Delta's 3Q26 earnings were 
TWD23.5bn (EPS: TWD9.04), missing our estimate of TWD27.3bn and 
Bloomberg consensus estimate of TWD29.6bn by 14% and 21%, 
respectively. We attribute the weakness mainly to lower-than-expected 
gross margin, driven by: 1) higher-than-expected inventory write-offs amid 
product shipment hiccups; 2) a one-off provision for customer claims, with 
the related loss likely to ultimately be borne by the supplier; and 3) rising 
material costs. Based on our calculation, Delta recognised c.TWD4bn of 
inventory provision loss in 3Q26, which reduced gross margin by 2pp. 
Excluding inventory provision loss, gross margin was c.35.8%, close to our 
previous estimate of 36.3%. 3Q26 revenue reached TWD197.1bn (up 8% 
QoQ and 31% YoY), which was also negatively affected by product 
shipment hiccups (vs. our previous estimate of 10% QoQ growth).

What we recommend: We trim our 2026-28E EPS by 2-6% to reflect lower 
revenue assumptions due to the impact of component shortages. We 
reaffirm our Buy (1) call but trim our 12M TP to TWD3,000 (from 
TWD3,088) based on an unchanged target PER of 49x (high-end of its 3-
year range of 11-50x) applied to our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. Key 
downside risk: lower-than-expected demand.

How we differ: Our 2028E revenue is 12% above the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely on our more bullish assumption regarding HVDC 
adoption.

29 October 2026

3Q26 review: margin likely bottomed-out

➢ Gross margin to recover on lower inventory provisions in 4Q26 
➢ Addressable market remains intact despite some near-term hiccups   
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating; trimming 12M TP to TWD3,000

Company profile 
Founded in 1971, Delta Electronics has been the global leader in switching power supply solutions since 
2002 and DC brushless fans since 2006. Its business covers 3 key areas: power electronics (eg, power 
supplies, thermal management products and passive components), automation (eg, controllers, robot 
arms and building management systems), and infrastructure (eg, datacentre infrastructure, networking 
and EV charging).

### Page 5

5

Delta Electronics (2308 TT): 29 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 30 Sep 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
Delta's board has 7 non-independent and 5 independent directors with a term of three years.
The total number of directors and ratio of independent directors appear higher than most other
listed companies. Besides, according to Delta's CSR report, enhancing the board's functions is
one of the key goals for the company to achieve better corporate governance; also, the board
members' diversity and professionalism will be taken into consideration to ensure a good board
quality. Moreover, the company evaluates all the directors in the board using internal regulations
and an external independent institution each year and at least once every three years,
respectively. We believe Delta has implemented relatively more enhanced measure than most
listed firms to improve its board quality.
Capital management 2
During 2016-19, Delta's payout ratio was stable in the range of c.70%, but fell to 56% in 2020
and 53% in 2021. For 2020, it was mainly due to a sizeable non-cash re-valuation gain after the
acquisition of Delta Thailand (DET), while the company maintained more cash on hand to deal
with the uncertainties caused by COVID-19. However, the DPS growth from 2016 to 2021 was
limited, as Delta struggled with business diversification into new end-markets (eg, smartphone,
NEV). We believe that Delta's effort started to bear fruit in recent years after its NEV business
has gradually picked up. Overall, we see a stable dividend policy from Delta and expect its DPS
to continue to grow YoY in the future given that they declared a TWD11.6 DPS for 2025 which
equals a 50% payout ratio.
Related party &
transaction 1 Revenue from related parties stood at 12% of total 2025 revenue and purchases from relatedparties were at 11%. We see limited risk from related party transactions.
S Supply chainmanagement 1
Delta focuses on: 1) localised management, 2) supplier certification and risk management, and
3) supplier sustainability engagement as key initiatives under the scope of supply chain
management. In 2019, 144 new suppliers joined Delta's supply chain system. All of them passed
environmental and social evaluations, signed the declaration of "non-use of conflict materials",
and complied with Responsible Business Alliance's (BRA) Code of Conduct. Also, these vendors
had to implement and abide by Delta's supply chain CSR policy and code of conduct. To lower
ESG risks, Delta carries out RBA audits and monitors high-risk critical suppliers each year. The
suppliers that fail these audits have to provide detailed improvement plans in response to
identified issues. Besides, Delta has surveyed a total of 263 suppliers with potential risk and 63
suppliers with high risk of the total 1,331 suppliers, based on its CSR policy and code of conduct.
If these suppliers are flagged as high-risk and no concrete improvements are made, Delta may
consider terminating cooperation and transactions with them to improve the company's
engagement on supplier sustainability. Overall, we think Delta has a solid plan to improve its
supply chain management with specific KPIs, including: 1) achieving supplier improvement rate
of 80% in 2025, 2) reducing GHG emissions rate of critical suppliers by 10% in 2025, and 3)
improving water saving rate of critical suppliers by 12% in 2025.
S Data security 2
Delta's Information Security team created Delta's Information Security Policy in November 2013
and started providing educational training to global employees in 2014. Delta gradually
introduced the Information Security Management System (ISMS) in 2016 and obtained the ISO
27001 international certification in 2018. According to ISO27001, Delta has to conduct at least
one internal audit on information security and appoint an independent institution to conduct audit
externally. According to Delta, no major deficiencies have been found in recent years.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
In this report, we focus on China's manufacturing PMI, which we
see as a potential catalyst for AirTAC's and Hiwin's share prices.
China's manufacturing PMI fell to 49.3 in October from 50.1 in September:

• This came in lower than both our estimate and consensus of 49.9.
• The production index decreased 1.9ppt while the new order index declined
1.1ppt MoM.
Our view:
• We believe the soft manufacturing PMI data could weigh on market
sentiment in the near term. Nonetheless, we prefer supply players with
company-specific drivers such as Airtac and Hiwin:
° Airtac (1590.TW, OW) should continue to benefit from its own share
gains and incremental contribution from linear guides. Valuation appears

undemanding at 16x 2027e P/E, vs. the 25x average since 2020 (see our
30 Oct 2026 report).
° Hiwin (2049.TW, OW) should show more margin expansion in coming
quarters, supported by higher utilization and price hikes. We see
potential share price upside at the current 28x 2027e P/E, vs. peak cycle
valuation of 35-40x.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
AirTAC International (1590.TW)
Base case, 29x 2027e P/E. We believe this methodology better captures earnings growth
momentum for industrial automation component plays (market sentiment moves along
with business cycles). As the industry enters an up-cycle, we think applying a near-peak cycle
valuation of 29x P/E is reasonable given the stronger business outlook.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger macroeconomic climate in China

n Stronger-than-expected demand from industrial automation
Risks to Downside
n Sharper economic downturn in China
n Miniature linear guideway business development taking more time
n Larger-than-expected investment in new products but weaker-than-expected demand
Hiwin Technologies Corp. (2049.TW)
Base case, P/E. We think this valuation methodology better captures Hiwin's earnings growth
momentum, as its valuation tends to move with the business cycle. We apply a target multiple

of 38x to our 2027 EPS estimate. We view this multiple as justified by the 47% operating profit
CAGR we project for 2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger industrial automation demand pickup.
n Market share gains.
n Earlier-than-expected revenue contribution from humanoids.
Risks to Downside
n Weaker industrial automation demand.
n More pricing pressure.
n Market share loss.
n More broad-based impact on the global economy from geopolitical risks.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 206: [000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 높아지는 이익의 눈높이, 달라지는 메모리 사이클SK하이닉스(000660) 높아지는 이익의 눈높이, 달라지는 메모리_0a1416f9

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 높아지는 이익의 눈높이, 달라지는 메모리 사이클SK하이닉스(000660) 높아지는 이익의 눈높이, 달라지는 메모리_0a1416f9.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29

Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

2026년 10월 29일 
기업분석

SK하이닉스 (000660) 
높아지는 이익의 눈높이, 달라지는 메모리 사이클

Buy(유지) 
목표주가 3,150,000원, 현재 주가(10/28): 1,487,000원 
▶ Analyst 박준영 jyp94@hanwha.com 3772-7481 / RA 김나우 now.kim@hanwha.com 3772-7710

3Q26 Review: HBM4 출하 확대와 예상보다 강한 가격 상승 
분기 매출액 104.6조원(QoQ +32%, YoY +328%), 영업이익 83.3조원(QoQ +38%, YoY +632%)을 기록하며 시장 기대
치를 상회함. 영업이익률은 전분기 대비 약 3%p 상승한 80%로 사상 최대 분기 실적을 달성함. 특히 DRAM ASP가 전
분기 대비 20% 후반 상승하며 당사 기존 예상치인 +23%를 상회한 점에 주목할 필요가 있음. NAND ASP 역시 20% 
중반 상승하며 예상보다 강한 가격 흐름을 나타냄. DRAM ASP 상승에는 기존 DRAM의 가격 인상과 함께 3분기부터 
본격화된 HBM4 양산 및 출하 확대에 따른 제품 믹스 개선 효과가 반영됨. 서버 DDR5 판매량 역시 전분기 대비 40% 
증가하며 실적 성장을 뒷받침함. 특히 HBM4는 기존 HBM3E 대비 높은 기술적 난도를 반영한 가격으로 공급되고 있어 
향후 출하 비중 확대에 따른 추가적인 ASP 상승 효과도 기대됨. 
이번 실적에서 중요한 부분은 예상보다 높은 가격 상승폭이 2027년 실적 추정치의 상향 가능성을 높이고 있다는 점임. 3
분기 DRAM ASP가 기존 추정치를 상회한 가운데 4분기에도 HBM4 출하 확대와 기존 DRAM의 가격 상승이 이어질 
것으로 예상되기 때문임. 동사는 4분기 DRAM 출하량이 전분기 대비 20% 중반 증가할 것으로 전망했으며, HBM4 출
하량이 HBM3E를 넘어 주력 제품으로 자리 잡을 것으로 예상함. 특히 HBM4의 양산 수율이 당초 목표를 상회하고 있
으며 높은 고객 수요에 대응하기 위해 생산성 극대화에 집중하고 있다는 점도 긍정적임. HBM4 출하 확대에 따른 믹스 
개선과 기존 DRAM 가격 상승이 동시에 진행되는 가운데 HBM과 일반 DRAM의 수익성 격차 역시 유의미하게 축소
될 것으로 예상됨. 이는 특정 제품에 대한 수익성 의존도가 낮아지면서 DRAM 사업 전반의 이익 체력이 강화되고 있음
을 의미함. 메모리 산업의 높은 영업 레버리지를 고려하면 예상보다 높은 ASP와 출하량 증가는 2027년 이익 추정치의 
추가 상향으로 연결될 가능성이 높다고 판단함.

공급 부족의 장기화와 LTA 확대, 메모리 산업의 이익 구조 변화 
이번 컨퍼런스콜에서 확인된 또 다른 중요한 변화는 공급 부족의 장기화와 장기공급계약(L TA) 확대임. 동사는 첨단 공정
의 기술적 난도 상승과 신규 생산시설 구축에 소요되는 시간을 고려할 때 메모리 공급이 수요를 충족하지 못하는 환경이 
2030년까지 지속될 것으로 전망함. 특히 글로벌 주요 고객사 14곳과 장기공급계약을 체결했으며, 2027~2028년 전체 생
산능력의 50% 이상에 해당하는 물량을 확보함. HBM의 경우 2029년까지의 물량이 이미 판매 확정된 상황으로 중장기 
수요 가시성이 상당히 높아졌다고 판단함. 주목할 부분은 L TA가 단순한 공급물량 확보를 넘어 메모리 업체의 사업 구조
와 이익 안정성을 변화시키고 있다는 점임. 계약 가격은 고객 및 제품별로 차이가 있으나 합의된 범위 내에서 시장 상황
을 반영하는 구조로 운영되고 있으며 예치금 등 실질적인 계약 이행력을 높이는 장치도 포함됨. 이는 장기적인 판매물량
을 확보하면서도 업황 상승에 따른 가격 인상 효과를 일정 부분 누릴 수 있다는 점에서 긍정적임.

### Page 2

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

2 
특히 전체 생산능력의 50% 이상을 L TA로 확보하면서 나머지 물량은 시장 상황에 따라 탄력적으로 대응할 수 있다는 점
에 주목할 필요가 있음. 장기계약을 통해 안정적인 매출 기반을 확보하는 동시에 미계약 물량을 활용해 추가적인 가격 
상승에 대응할 수 있기 때문임. 물론 장기계약이 메모리 가격 하락을 완전히 방어하는 것은 아니지만, 과거와 비교해 물
량 가시성과 고객사와의 협상력이 동시에 개선되고 있다는 점은 분명함. 과거 메모리 산업이 높은 이익을 창출하는 국면
에서도 낮은 밸류에이션을 적용 받았던 이유 중 하나는 이익의 지속성에 대한 불확실성이었음. 그러나 장기계약 확대와 
공급 제약의 장기화가 동시에 진행된다면 시장이 평가하는 이익의 지속성 역시 높아질 수 있음. 따라서 향후 주가 상승
은 단순한 EPS 추정치 상향뿐 아니라 이익 변동성 축소에 따른 밸류에이션 재평가가 동시에 진행되는 형태로 나타날 가
능성이 높다고 판단함. 
AI 인프라 투자에 대한 우려 역시 중장기 메모리 수요를 훼손할 가능성은 제한적이라고 판단함. 동사는 주요 하이퍼스케
일러의 클라우드 매출이 전년 대비 30~80% 성장하고 있으며 기업용 AI 솔루션 계약 확대와 클라우드 수주잔고 증가를 
통해 AI 투자의 사업적 성과가 확인되고 있다고 설명함. 특히 초기 AI 투자가 소수 빅테크에 집중되었던 것과 달리 최근
에는 일반기업의 자체 인프라 구축, 소버린 AI, 온프레미스 등으로 투자 주체가 다변화되고 있다는 점도 긍정적임. 특정 
사업자의 투자 속도 조절이 전체 AI 인프라 수요에 미치는 영향은 과거 대비 완화될 수 있기 때문임. AI 투자에 대한 우
려가 지속되는 상황에서도 실제 메모리 수요와 고객사들의 장기계약 확대가 확인되고 있다는 점에서 메모리 업황의 구조
적 성장에 대한 긍정적인 시각을 유지함.

HBM4 경쟁력 강화와 CAPEX 확대, 이익 성장과 주주환원의 선순환 
HBM4의 본격적인 출하 확대와 신규 생산능력 확보 역시 2027년 실적 성장의 중요한 기반이 될 전망임. 동사는 M15X
의 생산능력을 연말까지 최대 수준으로 확대할 계획이며 HBM4의 양산 수율 역시 당초 목표를 상회하는 등 안정적인 
생산체계를 확보하고 있음. 특히 HBM 수요가 공급능력의 두 배를 상회하는 상황에서 고객사들의 높은 선호가 이어지고 
있다는 점은 경쟁력 유지에 긍정적임. 최근 일부 고객사들이 HBM 적층 단수를 낮추는 방안을 검토하고 있으나 이는 공
급 부족 상황에서 제한된 물량을 효율적으로 활용하기 위한 전략으로 판단됨. 적층 단수 조정이 전체 HBM 수요 감소로 
직결되는 것은 아니며 오히려 후공정 생산능력에 대한 요구가 확대될 가능성도 존재함. HBM4E와 커스텀 HBM 개발 
역시 고객사 일정에 맞춰 진행되고 있어 차세대 제품에서도 경쟁력을 유지할 것으로 기대됨. 
한편 2027년 CAPEX는 선단 공정 전환과 용인 클러스터를 비롯한 국내외 생산 인프라 확충으로 전년 대비 증가할 전망
임. 다만 CAPEX 확대를 과거와 같은 공급 과잉의 신호로 해석할 필요는 없다고 판단함. 선단 공정의 기술적 난도 상승
과 고부가 제품 비중 확대에 따라 투자금액 증가가 실제 생산량 증가로 정비례하지 않기 때문임. 특히 동사는 신규 팹 
건설 일정을 앞당기면서도 클린룸은 순차적으로 개방하고 실제 생산능력 확대는 최종 수요와 수익성을 고려해 단계적으
로 진행할 계획임. 고객사의 투자 계획뿐 아니라 재무건전성과 자금조달 여력, 공급망 준비 상황까지 점검해 CAPEX 집
행에 반영한다는 점도 긍정적임. 이는 AI 인프라 투자 확대에 대응하면서도 과거 메모리 산업의 공급 과잉 위험을 최소
화하기 위한 전략으로 판단함. 
동사는 이에 더해 2026년 회계연도 기준 약 100조원 규모의 주주환원 계획을 발표했음. 현금배당 30조원 이상, 자사주 
매입 및 소각 60조원 이상으로 구성되며, 기존에 약속했던 잉여현금흐름(FCF)의 50% 이상을 주주에게 환원한다는 원칙
을 구체화한 것임. 지난 8월 발표한 40조원 규모의 자사주 매입은 10월 21일 완료됐으며, 해당 주식은 향후 소각될 예정
임. 이번 3분기 이사회에서는 현금배당 14조원과 추가 자사주 매입 20조원을 결의했으며, 자사주 매입액 중 10조원은 전
량 소각하고 나머지 10조원은 임직원 보상에 활용할 계획임. 임직원 보상용 자사주는 주주환원 규모에서 제외됨. 특히 
향후에도 자사주 매입을 지속할 계획이라는 점에서 주주환원 정책의 지속성이 강화됐다는 판단임.

### Page 3

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

3 
3분기 주당 배당금은 2만원으로, 실적 개선을 반영한 일반배당 1만원과 키옥시아 투자수익을 활용한 특별배당 1만원으
로 구성됨. 총 배당 규모는 약 14조원이며 회사는 올해 실현한 키옥시아 투자수익의 대부분을 3분기 및 연말 특별배당을 
통해 주주에게 환원할 계획임. 이번 결정은 메모리 가격 상승과 고부가 제품 확대에 따른 현금창출력 개선이 실질적인 
주주환원 확대로 연결되고 있음을 보여줌. 특히 2027년 설비투자 확대가 예상되는 상황에서도 대규모 현금배당과 자사
주 소각을 병행한다는 점은 재무적 여력에 대한 자신감을 반영하는 것으로 판단됨. 향후에도 안정적인 실적 성장과 FCF 
확대가 이어질 경우 주주환원의 지속 가능성이 높아질 전망이며 이는 과거 대비 높아진 이익 창출력과 함께 중장기 밸류
에이션 재평가를 뒷받침하는 요인으로 작용할 것으로 예상됨. 
결국 이번 실적발표에서 확인된 핵심은 예상보다 강한 메모리 가격 상승과 HBM4의 본격적인 출하 확대, 그리고 장기계
약을 통한 이익 가시성 개선임. 특히 HBM4의 높은 가격과 안정적인 양산 수율, 기존 DRAM의 가격 상승이 동시에 확
인되면서 2027년 이익 추정치의 추가 상향 가능성이 높아졌다고 판단함. 여기에 L TA 확대에 따른 중장기 이익 안정성 
개선과 대규모 주주환원까지 더해지고 있음. 실적 추정치 상향과 이익의 지속성 개선이 동시에 진행되고 있다는 점에서 
긍정적인 투자 시각을 유지함.

### Page 4

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

4

본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 자료를 
기관투자자 또는 제3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (박준영, 김나우) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 5

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

5 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 09 월 30일)

◼ 3Q26 파업 영향에도 컨센서스 상회 
◼ 4Q26 지연 물량 인식 및 신규 공장 기여로 실적 개선 전망 
◼ 투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
목표주가는 DCF 방식으로 산출(WACC 8.1%, 영구성장률 3.5% 적용). 기
존 공장의 높은 가동률을 기반으로 5공장 매출 기여 확대, 미국 생산거점 
확보, 신규 모달리티 진출을 통한 중장기 성장 지속 전망.

3Q26 Review: 파업 영향에도 컨센서스 상회  
3Q26 매출액 1조 4,903억원(+18.5% yoy, +12.8% qoq), 영업이익 6,401
억원(+1.1% yoy, +9.1% qoq), 영업이익률 42.9%(-7.5%p yoy)를 기록하며 
당사 추정치 및 시장 컨센서스 상회. 5월 1차 파업에 따른 매출 차질(약 
1,500억원) 에도 불구하고, 기존 1~4공장 Full 가동이 지속되며 견조한 실
적 달성. 5공장 PPQ 배치 생산 확대와 및 미국 록빌 공장 매출 인식도 외
형 성장에 기여. 다만, 신규 생산시설 초기 가동비와 제품 믹스 영향으로 
영업이익률은 전년 동기 대비 하락.

4Q26 매출액 1조 5,764억원(+22.6% yoy), 영업이익 7,075억원
(+34.0% yoy) 전망  
4Q26에는 2분기 파업으로 지연된 생산물량의 순차적인 매출 인식과 미국 
록빌 공장 매출 기여, 5공장 PPQ 배치 생산 확대로 실적 개선 예상, 동사
는 신규 생산시설의 매출 가시성 확보를 바탕으로 2026년 매출 성장률 
20%를 무난히 달성할 것으로 전망(연초 가이던스 15~20%).

신규 수주 회복과 포트폴리오 확장에 주목 
미국 의약품 관세정책 불확실성 완화로 지연됐던 수주 협상이 재개되며 신
규 수주 환경 개선 중. 3Q26 약 3억달러의 신규 수주를 확보했으며, 대형 
계약 협상도 구체화되고 있어 2027년 수주 회복 기대. 이는 5공장 가동률 
확대와 중장기 성장 가시성을 높이는 요인. 6공장은 ‘26년말~’27년초 착
공을 목표로 기존 증설 일정 유지. 
폴리펩타이드 그룹 인수는 1차 공개매수 응모율 87.88%를 기록하며 연내 
인수 완료 추진 중. 인수 완료 시 펩타이드 CDMO로 사업영역이 확대되며 
글로벌 생산거점 및 고객 기반 다변화 기대.

## Document 285: Blue Wave_ Democrats sweep the US midterms

Source PDF: all report pdf/Blue Wave_ Democrats sweep the US midterms.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Market Watch)
Date: 2026-11-05
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Blue Wave: Democrats 
sweep the US midterms  
Summary 
Event: The Democrats gained control of the House of 
Representatives and the Senate at the US midterm elections 
that took place this week.  
Our View: With the US government now no longer under 
unified control of the Republicans, gridlock has returned to 
Washington. We believe investors should not overreact to 
these results and should stay invested. 
In equities, investors should  continue to diversify their 
equity exposure into more defensive sectors.  As we 
transition to mid -cycle for the equity markets , we expect 
stocks to continue to deliver positive returns in the 8 -10% 
range per annum, a downshift from the torrid pace of gains in 
the earlier part of the cycle.  
In bonds, income remains attractive given recent spread 
widening. We favour non -hyperscaler investment grade 
corporate bonds at the three to seven -year part of the 
curve. We continue to expect the US dollar to remain range-
bound in the near  term, as we expect two more rate hikes 
from the Fed, but as this mini rate hike cycle ends in the 
second quarter of 2027, the USD index (DXY) is likely to 
depreciate towards 98.5 as markets start to anticipate a Fed 
rate cut in the second half of next year.  
Oil is likely to rebound in the short -term on heightened 
geopolitical risk, but as supply chains adapt to the current 
situation and Congress exercises oversight on defense 
spending, WTI prices are likely to drift towards our 12-month 
target of USD 80 per barrel.

Democrats made solid gains across the Senate, House 
of Representatives and State Governors 
Results reported as at 7:45PM Singapore Time

Source: The Wall Street Journal, Standard Chartered

### Page 2

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 5 November 2026

Major implications of Democrat control of 
Congress   
1) Fiscal expansionary policies may slow. Democrats are 
now in position to block any new tax cuts or spending plans 
from the Trump administration. At the same time, any fiscal 
policies proposed by Democrats may also face Trump’s veto. 
Hence the fiscal trajectory of the US will likely remain stuck  
over the next two years. As we get into the second half of 
2027, the US debt ceiling will once again loom, and gridlock 
could potentially bring back government shutdowns. 
However, as they have always done in the past, compromise 
will ultimately prevail, in our opinion.  
2) Trump remains in control of foreign policy.  With the 
midterms now out of the way, could Trump decide to re-start 
combat operations with Iran? We believe there is a risk that 
US and Israel – now that both countries’ have concluded 
important elections – will once again be tempted to restart 
combat operations to force an end to the conflict. However, 
the duration of any renewed hostilities may be limited by 
Congress next year as the Democrats will likely withhold 
funding for further military operations. In the near -term, oil 
prices may experience renewed volatility before stabilising at 
a lower level next year. The introduction of new tariffs on 
trading partners may prove harder with Democrat oversight. 
3) AI data centre moratoriums could grow, though model 
development regulatory changes would be harder to 
pass. While the headlines have focused on the House and 
Senate results, one important development has also 
occurred at the gubernatorial level. Notably, Democrats have 
won the governor races in Texas and Georgia, two big data 
centre states. As data centre build out permits are largely 
decided at the local state level, this will have important 
ramifications for build out plans next year.  However, any 
Democratic proposals to regulate AI model developments 
would need to be signed off by President Trump, who 
continues to wield veto power on any such proposals.   
Asset class strategy 
Equities: Remain invested and continue to diversify .  
While investors initially overreacted, President Trump’s 
relatively conciliatory response has provided some welcome 
relief, helping markets recover strongly from their pre-market 
lows. Hence, we recommend investors avoid 
overreacting, remain invested and continue to diversify 
their equity exposure across sectors and regions.  
A few themes stand out from the early pre-market reaction to 
the mid-term elections. On the positive side, defensive stocks 
continue to fare well, while themes favoured by Democrats 
are also outperforming. Clean energy is a notable 
beneficiary, with solar s tocks among the biggest winners

Policy uncertainty and a change in the innovation cycle 
is consistent with our view of a mid-cycle transition, 
where equity returns moderate but remain positive

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 5 November 2026

today. Affordability-related themes are also rallying, including 
companies that could benefit from potential tariff rollbacks. 
Medicaid-related stocks are also moving higher on 
expectations that potential policy changes could provide a 
tailwind for the healthcare sector. 
On the negative side, semiconductor weakness continues 
amid concerns around potential data centre moratoriums. 
Our AI capex estimates remain conservative relative to 
consensus, but with a potential reset in expectations around 
data centre spending, investo rs should remain selective. 
After downgrading semiconductors last month, we have 
maintained a conservative stance on the industry, with a 
lower recommended allocation compared to September. 
While we believe the mid -term election outcome is not a 
complete surprise to markets, particularly given pre -election 
polling and the recent weakness in both the S&P 500 and 
Nasdaq, the overnight correction highlights how complacent 
investors had become, with the VIX initially surging more than 
10% in pre -market trading, although down from intra -day 
highs after President Trump’s comments. 
As we have highlighted in our recent notes on the transition 
to mid-cycle, this phase is typically characterised by greater 
political headwinds . T he mid -term election  outcome 
reinforces the trend. The experience of 2015 -2017, 
highlighted in the second table, offers a useful historical 
parallel. Despite the knee-jerk reaction, investors should not 
overreact based on one day’s moves. While we had trimmed 
equities to a core allocation last week, we continue to expect 
equities to deliver mid-to-high single-digit returns per annum 
during the mid -cycle, even amid political headwinds, 
supported by steady underlying earnings growth in both 2027 
and 2028. 
So, what should investors do to navigate the next few months 
before the newly elected representatives are sworn in and 
take control of the House and Senate?  As we have been 
highlighting in recent notes, equity markets should still deliver 
positive returns, albeit at a more moderate pace. Hence, we 
recommend staying invested but diversification remains key  
to navigating the next phase of the cycle.  
FX: DXY near-term support at 100.7, 12 -month target at 
98.5. A Democratic sweep potentially removes the legislative 
runway for further tariff escalation and for fiscal expansion, 
the two structural pillars of US exceptionalism that have 
anchored dollar demand in 2026. Reduced fiscal impulse 
compresses the growth and yield differential that has kept the 
US dollar well-bid all year. Additionally, potential Democratic 
restrictions on AI and data centre investment could weigh on 
US equity outperformance, which is likely to act as a 
secondary dollar-negative channel.  
However, despite historical readings supporting a near -term 
DXY headwind from a Democratic sweep, the magnitude will

likely be modest and short -lived unless compounded by a 
weaker than expected non-farm payrolls report on Friday. We 
see the Fed narrative, not the election, as the dominant 
medium-term driver. Near -term we see USD index (DXY) 
support at 100.7, and retain our 12-month target at 98.5.  
Fixed Income: Still attractive all -in yields ; prefer 
investment grade corporates at three -to-seven year  
tenors to earn income and manage interest rate risk. The 
US Treasury bond market reaction appears to reflect a 
moderation in expectations of future economic growth after 
the election results. Yields at the intermediate end  of the 
curve dropped the most, while the 30 -year US yield 
moderated more modestly. We continue to expect two more 
Fed rate hikes between now and H1 2027 as still sticky 
inflation and the robust economy support further tightening.  
In the past few weeks, the US Treasury yield curve has 
broadly shifted downwards, after our opportunistic call to go 
long 10-year Treasuries at the end of September. We are of 
the view the ultra-long end downshift in yields is overdone 
(given the still challenging US fiscal trajectory) and would 
recommend investors who had earlier taken long duration 
exposures at the 20 to 30-year parts of the curve reduce their 
positions now. We continue to believe the 3 to 7-year part of 
the curve offers the most optimal balance of income and 
interest rate risk, particularly via investment corporate bonds. 
Within corporate credit, we expect a potentially slower pace 
of AI data centre buildouts could marginally improve market 
technicals, as hyperscaler and tech sector bond issuance 
may slow  (and reduce “crowding out” of other bond 
issuances). Conversely, from a credit perspective, we believe 
this dynamic may also make the data centre focused deals 
less viable, and  would be cautious on some of this sector’s 
lower quality names. Meanwhile, less AI capex dependent 
issuers continue to offer solid credit profiles amidst the still 
strong economy. High yield corporate spreads have widened 
in recent weeks but dispersion wi thin the complex is wide, 
with BBs and Bs credit spreads staying relatively resilient, 
while CCCs have widened to distressed levels. For income -
focused investors, we would recommend staying within the 
crossover range of between BBB-BB.  
Commodities: As noted in the opening section, we believe 
there is a higher near -term risk of a restart of combat 
operations in Iran given the conclusion of the midterm 
elections. Given that, we believe there is a risk that oil 
prices could see a short -term spike , despite recent 
diplomatic overtures from Iran. However, for 2027 we 
continue to expect oil to stabilise at lower levels , as we 
think the Mideast situation should de -escalate given 
Democratic oversight of the defense budget, and the likely re-
configuration of oil supply chains. On the margin, we are also 
closely monitoring regulatory developments in the US shale

We expect oil prices to drift towards USD 80 a barrel as 
oil supply chains adapt, and Congress restrains the US 
defense budget

oil industry as the Democrats may seek to introduce new

On gold, we view the recent change in control in the 
Congress as only having a marginal impact  and thus 
stay Overweight . USD strength is a headwind (which is 
more dependent on Fed policy), but gold’s sensitivity to real 
yields has eased amid continued central bank buying, led by 
China, and resilient investor demand.  
— Jonathan Liang, CFA, CIO, Fixed Income & FX

## Document 315: Cotality-Westpac NZ First Home Buyer Report, Oct 2026 (2026-10-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cotality-Westpac NZ First Home Buyer Report, Oct 2026 (2026-10-01).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 19
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

October 2026
First Home
Buyer Report

### Page 2

2 © 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
Cotality’s
First Home Buyer
(FHB) Report has tracked
purchasing activity for
several years,
analysing
the
types of properties bought,
the prices paid, and broader
shifts in entry
-
level demand.
The latest edition updates
these measures and extends
the analysis to examine FHB
activity across different
price brackets.
Now in its fourth edition in
partnership with Westpac,
the report also draws on the

bank’s mortgage lending
data to provide additional
insight into borrower
characteristics. That
includes the average age of
FHB borrowers, a measure
not widely available
elsewhere, as well as loan
-
to
-
value ratios across new
mortgages.
For clarity, we define a first home
buyer purchase to be where no party
involved has owned property in New
Zealand before and are using a
mortgage to make the deal.
Note:
The Westpac FHB age and LVR data
are sourced from their lending records

and is not matched to the Cotality
database for this report.
Cotality – Westpac
First Home Buyer Report

### Page 3

3
© 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
FHBs’ market share remains
at record high levels.
In Q3
2026, they accounted for
29.5% of property purchases
across NZ, surpassing the
previous mark of 28.3% only
set three months earlier. The
number of deals remains at
historically high levels too,
bucking the wider trend of
slowing sales volumes for
groups such as investors and
relocating owner
-
occupiers.
This strength for FHBs is
being seen right across the

country,
with market shares
generally running above their
own long
-
term averages
(since 2005). The wider
Wellington area remains a
hotspot for FHBs, accounting
for 37% of activity so far in
2026. But FHBs have also
been at least 30% of the
market in Hamilton and
Auckland, as well as Gisborne,
Rotorua, Hastings,
Palmerston North, and
Invercargill
–
South Waikato
and Waikato District too.
This high level of activity
hasn’t meant too many
compromises either;

standalone houses have
comprised 76% of all FHB
purchases so far in 2026, up
from 75% last year, and the
highest figure since 2020
(78%). To be fair, FHBs have
had to pay a little more, with a
median price so far this year
of $715,000, but still less than
nearly $719,000 in 2022.
FHBs are more active across
the value spectrum too.
For
example, in the middle 40% of
the market by value, their
share rose from 21% in 2015 to
28% in 2025, and now about

30% so far this year.
Access to low deposit
finance under the LVR rules
remains a key support for
first home buyers.
Recent
Reserve Bank data shows that
more than half of FHB loans
are done at less than 20%
deposit (or above 80% LVR),
and Westpac’s own lending
records show a recent
average LVR for FHBs of a
touch more than 80%.
The average age for FHBs on
Westpac’s books has ticked
higher lately, from around 35
late last year to about 35.5
now.
But rather than

necessity due to affordability
pressures, this is more likely
to reflect a choice from many
buyers to just wait for the
right property without fear the
market will run away again in
the meantime.
Looking ahead, although it’s
never easy to get that first
home, conditions should
remain
favourable
for FHBs.
Mortgage rates remain lower
than at many points in the
past, KiwiSaver access for at
least part of the deposit
remains a strong support, and
the banks remain willing and

able to lend at low
deposit/high LVR too.
This is clearly a ‘good
new
s
story’ which we should never
get blasé or complacent
about,
after so many previous
years (even decades) of
intense housing affordability
pressures and a declining
home ownership rate.
As we head into November’s
election, the bottom line is
that first home buyers are
starting from a strong base.
Key findings
Cotality – Westpac
First Home Buyer Report

### Page 4

4
© 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
It’s been another solid few months
for first home buyers with their share
of house sales remaining around
record highs across the country.
That’s a stark contrast to the
softness in the broader housing
market over the past year.
Helping to support those high levels
of first home buyers have been a
couple of big trends. The first is the
large number of properties available
for sale. That’s meant those looking

to get their foot on the property
ladder have had plenty of options. As
a result, first home buyers have often
been able to get into newer and
larger standalone homes without
having to pay significantly more.
The other factor that has boosted
first home buyer numbers has been
the relatively low level of mortgage
rates in recent years. Combined with
broadly flat house prices, that’s
meant servicing a mortgage has
been much more affordable, with the

share of disposable income being
spen
t
on debt
-
servicing costs back
around pre
-
pandemic averages. For
the average first home buyer, lower
mortgage interest rates in recent
years shaved around $560 off their
monthly mortgage payments
compared to 2023 when interest
rates were at their peak. And in
Auckland, where first homes tend to
be more expensive, the saving is
around $685 a month.
While those are encouraging trends
for first home buyers, there are some

big changes in economic conditions
occurring which are likely to have
important implications for the
housing market over the year ahead.
Most notable has been the sharp rise
in inflation in the wake of the Middle
East war, with oil prices rocketing
higher since the start of this year.
That contributed to consumer prices
rising by 3.7% in the year to
September. Relatively high core
inflation levels mean we expect
inflation will push above 4% before

the end of this year and remain high
for much of 2027.
In response to those mounting
inflation pressures, and concerns
that they will persist for some time,
the RBNZ began lifting the Official
Cash Rate (OCR) back in July. We
expect further hikes in coming
months. The resulting pressure on
borrowing costs has seen mortgage
rates push higher in recent months,
with one
-
and two
-
year fixed rates
up around 0.75 percentage points
over the past year.

Even with the recent rise in
mortgage rates, the housing market
is still a lot more accessible for first
home buyers than a few years ago.
But with growing upward pressure
on interest rates, it’s a good time for
prospective first home buyers (or
anyone with a re
-
fix coming up) to
think about the best way to structure
their borrowing. That could help
insulate them from the likely trend
higher in the OCR over the next
couple of years and could save them

a lot of money over the life of their
mortgage. There’s a relatively
modest difference between short
-
and longer
-
term fixed mortgage
rates right now, which means the
cost of taking this ‘insurance’ is not
prohibitive.
Commentary from Satish Ranchhod, Westpac NZ Senior Economist

Average age of first home buyers from Westpac loans (2018 to 2026)
Providing some offset to the lift in
borrowing costs, house price
inflation is low, with effectively no
growth in average house prices for
three years now. And with mortgage
rates pushing higher, prices are
expected to remain soft for some
time. We expect that average house
prices will fall by 1.4% over 2026,
with a rise of just 1.8% in 2027.

At the same time, the supply of
housing is continuing to lift, with
more than 40,000 new dwellings
consented over the past year.  We
expect population growth to
increase, but from low levels, which
means the demand for housing might
not be shifting sharply higher any
time soon.
That combination of conditions
means that there is still likely to be a
lot of options
f
or
those looking to get
into their first home over the coming
year.

### Page 6

6
© 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
The national picture
Since our previous FHB Report
back in May 2026, the wider
housing market has remained
subdued and patchy
–
generally
sales volumes have been flat to
falling, while property values have
been showing a similarly soft
picture, albeit not everywhere.
Amongst the main
centres
,
Auckland and Wellington, as
examples, have been more sluggish
than say Christchurch or Dunedin.
It’s also noteworthy that the stock

of listings available for sale remains
elevated across the country.
It’s not difficult to find the reasons
why the market remains in the
doldrums.
The economic backdrop
is still uncertain, the unemployment
rate is above average, and
mortgage rates have been drifting
higher in recent months as inflation
fears remain strong. A better
balance of physical housing supply
versus our population is also
dampening prices, while the
election has been a recent reason

for caution too. It’s harder to
measure, but ‘animal spirits’ aren’t a
key factor
at the moment
either.
FHBs have gone from strength to
strength
In this purchaser
-
friendly
environment, first home buyers
have continued to take full
advantage.
The Cotality Buyer
Classification data shows that their
market share hit a new quarterly
record high of 28.3% in Q2 2026,
only to push higher still in Q3
–
reaching 29.5%. They’re also faring
well in terms of the number of deals

too, recently hovering at around
25,000 on a 12
-
month rolling basis,
back to levels last seen (for a brief
period) at the height of the post
-
COVID boom.
Clearly, lower property values
–
still down by more than 18%
nationally from the peak
–
and
improved housing affordability
(even despite some recent rises in
mortgage rates) are helping FHBs.
But those aren’t the only factors;
access to (ever
-
growing) KiwiSaver
pots for at least part of the deposit

is also a factor, alongside decent
availability of high LVR finance at
the banks.
Indeed, the 25% allowance/speed
limit to lend to owner
-
occupiers at
less than 20% equity (or >80% LVR)
is not being tested
at the moment
and recently more than half of FHB
loans have been advanced at less
than 20% deposit.
Westpac’s own
mortgage lending records show
this in a different way
–
with the
average LVR for their loans to
FHBs in recent months still
running a touch above 80%.

LVRs
tend to be higher in Auckland, but a
bit lower in Canterbury and
Wellington.
They’re also getting more for their
money
FHBs have always tended to be
willing to compromise on location
or property type to make the shift
from renting into ownership, but
lately there hasn’t been much need
to take such an approach.
Over
2026 to date (Q3), standalone
houses have comprised 76% of all
FHB purchases, up from 75% last
year, and well above 2023’s
cyclical trough of less than 70%.

For comparison, standalone houses
have accounted for around 72% of
deals across all
buyers
year to date.
N
ational
FHB activity

### Page 8

8
      © 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
This shows that FHBs are getting 
the most for their money in a 
quieter market. Admittedly, the 
median price they’ve paid so far 
this year has risen to $715,000, up 
from $701,000 in 2025 
 and also  
higher than the 2023 ($695,000) 
and 2024 figures ($692,000). But 
that latest price is still around 
$4,000 lower than in 2022, yet 
FHBs are still getting ‘more house’.
Another important point to make is 
that FHBs don’t always enter at the 
bottom of the housing ladder and 
work their way up 
 – 
many 
 actually 
enter  
well above the lowest tiers of 
the market. Indeed, the FHB 
median price paid of $715,000 this 
year is below the all
 -
 buyer figure of 
$785,000, but well above the all
 -
buyer lower quartile (bottom 25%) 
of $595,000. 
This point can be made another 
way too, with Cotality figures 
showing a rising share of activity 
for FHBs right across the price 
spectrum. Perhaps most notably, 
FHBs have accounted for 30% of 
purchases in the middle 40% price 
bracket of transactions so far this 
year, up from a 28% share in 2025, 
25% in 2020, and only 21% in 2015. 
They’ve even accounted for 15% of 
activity so far this year in the top 
30% of deals split by price.
FHBs’ average age has edged 
higher again, but probably just due 
to a lack of pressure
Meanwhile, at face value, 
Westpac’s data showing that the 
average age of 
 a 
FHB has recently 
edged back up to around 2024 
levels of 35.5 again, after a trough 
of 35 late last year, could seem at 
odds with the other, more 
encouraging trends. But rather than 
being out of necessity (
 e.g.  
due to 
affordability pressures), this could 
reflect the abundance of listings 
and subdued house prices, 
meaning that 
 some/many FHBs 
might just be choosing to wait a 
bit longer to get their ideal 
property, without fear they’ll be 
left behind by a rising market.

A solid outlook
Overall, it’s never easy to buy that 
first home and FHBs certainly 
continue to face challenges. But 
their presence has remained strong 
in recent months and, although 
nothing lasts forever, it’s not hard 
to imagine that FHBs stay a solid 
presence into 2027 as well. This is 
a ‘good 
 news 
 story
 ’ which we 
should never get 
 blasé 
 or 
complacent about, after so many 
previous years (decades) of intense 
housing affordability pressures and 
a declining home ownership rate.

The main centres
Looking at the data across
Auckland, Hamilton, Tauranga,
wider Wellington (i.e. combined
City, Lower Hutt, Upper Hutt,
Porirua), Christchurch, and
Dunedin, the national trends can
also be seen at the more granular
level
–
indeed, all of these areas
have had FHB market shares lately
that are above their own averages.
The wider Wellington area remains
a standout for FHBs, where their

share so far this year has been a
touch above 37%, well
in excess of
their long
-
term average in that
market of 30%. FHBs have also had
a market share in 2026 above 30%
in Hamilton and Auckland. The
figures have been a little lower in
Christchurch and Dunedin (both
around 28%), as well as Tauranga
(circa 25%), but in each of those
markets the figures have still been
4
-
8%
-
points above their own
averages.
Looking at the different property
types, standalone houses still

dominate in most areas, accounting
for 85
-
90% of FHB purchases in
Dunedin, Tauranga, and Hamilton
–
broadly in line with the
long term
norms in those areas. The share for
houses amongst FHB purchases is
always lower in Christchurch,
Wellington, and Auckland, but it’s
perhaps the former that stands out
most at present
–
with only 66% of
FHB purchases so far in 2026 in
Christchurch being standalone
houses, versus the historical
average of 75%. This no doubt

reflects the recent townhouse
construction upswing.
In each main
centre
, the median
FHB price is lower than the figure
for all buyers, but higher than the
lower quartile for all buyers
–
in
other words, FHBs don’t always
enter at the bottom. In terms of the
prices themselves, Auckland is the
main
centre
where FHBs pay the
most, at a median of $880,000 so
far in 2026, which is $110,000 less
than the all
-
buyer median, but
$110,000 more than the all

-
buyer
lower quartile. Median FHB prices
paid are $795,500 in Tauranga so
far in 2026, followed by Wellington
at $736,250, Hamilton $715,000,
and lower still in Christchurch and
Dunedin.
